```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react'
4	import { t, Language } from './translations'
5	
6	type LanguageContextType = {
7	  lang: Language
8	  setLang: (lang: Language) => void
9	  tx: typeof t.en
10	}
11	
12	const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType>({
13	  lang: 'en',
14	  setLang: () => {},
15	  tx: t.en,
16	})
17	
18	export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
19	  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Language>('en')
20	
21	  useEffect(() => {
22	    const saved = localStorage.getItem('yff_lang') as Language
23	    if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'te') setLangState(saved)
24	  }, [])
25	
26	  const setLang = (newLang: Language) => {
27	    setLangState(newLang)
28	    localStorage.setItem('yff_lang', newLang)
29	  }
30	
31	  return (
32	    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, tx: t[lang] }}>
33	      {children}
34	    </LanguageContext.Provider>
35	  )
36	}
37	
38	export function useLang() {
39	  return useContext(LanguageContext)
40	}
41	
```

68	  const [available, setAvailable]     = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
69	  const [comingSoon, setComingSoon]   = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
70	  const [filtered, setFiltered]       = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])

79	  const fetchData = async () => {
80	    setLoading(true)
81	    try {
82	      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([
83	        fetch('/api/produce'),
84	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon'),
85	      ])
86	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
87	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
88	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
89	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []

91	      setFiltered(avArr)
92	      setComingSoon(csArr)
93	      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)

248	/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */
249	function ProduceCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
250	  const emoji    = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
251	  const emojiBg  = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
252	  const method   = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'
253	  const badge    = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
254	  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'
255	  const farmer   = item.farmer
256	  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'
257	
258	  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()
259	  const inCart = cart[item.id]
260	
261	  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone
262	
263	  const handleAdd = () => {
264	    if (!farmer) return
265	    addItem({
266	      listingId: item.id,
267	      name: item.name,
268	      variety: item.variety,
269	      emoji: item.emoji,
270	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
271	      farmerId: farmer.id,
272	      farmerName: farmer.name,
273	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
274	      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
275	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
276	    }, 1)
277	  }
278	
279	  return (
280	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col">
281	      {/* Emoji header — taps go to farmer profile */}
282	      <Link href={farmerHref}>
283	        <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
284	          <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
285	        </div>
286	      </Link>
287	
288	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
289	        {/* Name + variety */}
290	        <Link href={farmerHref}>
291	          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
292	          {item.variety && (
293	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{item.variety}</p>
294	          )}
295	        </Link>
296	
297	        {/* Farmer info */}
298	        {farmer && (
299	          <Link href={farmerHref} className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
300	            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />
301	            📍 {farmer.village}
302	          </Link>
303	        )}
304	
305	        {/* Price + method badge */}
306	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
307	          <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
308	            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}
309	            <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/kg</span>
310	          </span>
311	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>
312	            {badgeLabel}
313	          </span>
314	        </div>
315	
316	        {/* Stock */}
317	        {item.stock_qty != null && (
318	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
319	            {item.stock_qty} kg left / మిగిలాయి
320	          </p>
321	        )}
322	
323	        {/* CTA */}
324	        {!canAdd ? (
325	          <Link
326	            href={farmerHref}
327	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
328	          >
329	            View / చూడండి
330	          </Link>
331	        ) : !inCart ? (
332	          <button
333	            onClick={handleAdd}
334	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
335	          >
336	            + Add / చేర్చు
337	          </button>
338	        ) : (
339	          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5">
340	            <button
341	              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1)}
342	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold"
343	              aria-label="Decrease"
344	            >
345	              −
346	            </button>
347	            <span className="font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm">
348	              {inCart.qty} kg
349	            </span>
350	            <button
351	              onClick={() => setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)}
352	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-green-700 text-white text-lg font-bold"
353	              aria-label="Increase"
354	            >
355	              +
356	            </button>
357	          </div>
358	        )}
359	      </div>
360	    </div>
361	  )
362	}

432	/* ─── Demand intent banner ──────────────────────────────────── */
433	function DemandIntentBanner() {
434	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
435	  const [crop, setCrop] = useState('')
436	  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
437	  const [date, setDate] = useState('')
438	  const [location, setLocation] = useState('')
439	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
440	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
441	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
442	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
443	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
444	
445	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
446	    if (!crop.trim() || !name.trim() || !phone.trim()) {
447	      setError('Please fill crop name, your name, and phone number.')
448	      return
449	    }
450	    setLoading(true)
451	    setError('')
452	    const { supabase: sb } = await import('@/lib/supabase')
453	    const { error: err } = await sb.from('demand_intents').insert({
454	      crop_name: crop.trim(),
455	      quantity_kg: qty ? Number(qty) : null,
456	      needed_by_date: date || null,
457	      delivery_location: location.trim() || null,
458	      requester_name: name.trim(),
459	      requester_phone: phone.trim(),
460	      region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',
461	    })
462	    setLoading(false)
463	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
464	    setSubmitted(true)
465	  }
466	
467	  return (
468	    <div className="px-4 mt-10 mb-4">
469	      <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
470	        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
471	          <span className="text-2xl">📣</span>
472	          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
473	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">Raise a Demand Intent</h3>
474	            <p className="text-amber-700 text-sm mt-1 leading-snug">
475	              Let local farmers know what you need — they&apos;ll reach out when available.
476	            </p>
477	            <p className="text-amber-600 text-xs mt-0.5">
478	              స్థానిక రైతులకు మీకు ఏమి కావాలో తెలియజేయండి
479	            </p>
480	          </div>
481	        </div>
482	
483	        {!open && !submitted && (
484	          <button
485	            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
486	            className="mt-4 w-full bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
487	          >
488	            <span className="text-lg leading-none">+</span>
489	            Raise Intent / డిమాండ్ నమోదు
490	          </button>
491	        )}
492	
493	        {submitted && (
494	          <div className="mt-4 text-center py-3">
495	            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold text-base">✓ Intent raised!</p>
496	            <p className="text-green-600 text-sm mt-1">Farmers in your area will be notified.</p>
497	          </div>
498	        )}
499	
500	        {open && !submitted && (
501	          <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
502	            <input
503	              type="text"
504	              placeholder="Crop name * (e.g. Tomato / టమాటా)"
505	              value={crop}
506	              onChange={(e) => setCrop(e.target.value)}
507	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
508	            />
509	            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
510	              <input
511	                type="number"
512	                placeholder="Quantity (kg)"
513	                value={qty}
514	                onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
515	                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
516	              />
517	              <input
518	                type="date"
519	                value={date}
520	                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
521	                className="border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
522	              />
523	            </div>
524	            <input
525	              type="text"
526	              placeholder="Delivery location (e.g. Tadepalligudem)"
527	              value={location}
528	              onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
529	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
530	            />
531	            <input
532	              type="text"
533	              placeholder="Your name *"
534	              value={name}
535	              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
536	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
537	            />
538	            <input
539	              type="tel"
540	              placeholder="Your WhatsApp number *"
541	              value={phone}
542	              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
543	              className="w-full border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
544	            />
545	
546	            {error && (
547	              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
548	            )}
549	
550	            <div className="flex gap-2">
551	              <button
552	                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setError('') }}
553	                className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-600 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
554	              >
555	                Cancel
556	              </button>
557	              <button
558	                onClick={handleSubmit}
559	                disabled={loading}
560	                className="flex-1 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
561	              >
562	                {loading ? 'Submitting...' : 'Submit / నమోదు'}
563	              </button>
564	            </div>
565	          </div>
566	        )}
567	      </div>
568	    </div>
569	  )
570	}

40	  !!f && f.name?.trim().length > 0 && f.village?.trim().length > 0
41	
42	export default function FarmerDashboard() {
43	  const router = useRouter()
44	  const [farmer, setFarmer] = useState<Farmer | null>(null)
45	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
46	  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false)
47	  const [activeListings, setActiveListings] = useState(0)
48	  const [ordersCount, setOrdersCount] = useState(0)
49	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
50	  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
51	  const [showProfileEdit, setShowProfileEdit] = useState(false)
52	
53	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
54	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
55	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
56	
57	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
58	      .from('farmers')
59	      .select('*')
60	      .eq('id', farmerId)
61	      .maybeSingle()
62	
63	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
64	    setFarmer(farmerData)
65	
66	    const [listingsRes, ordersRes, intentsRes] = await Promise.all([
67	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),
68	      supabase.from('orders').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
69	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
70	    ])
71	
72	    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)
73	    setOrdersCount(ordersRes.count ?? 0)
74	
75	    const map: Record<string, number> = {}
76	    for (const row of intentsRes.data ?? []) {
77	      map[row.crop_name] = (map[row.crop_name] ?? 0) + (Number(row.quantity_kg) || 0)
78	    }
79	    setDemandBars(
80	      Object.entries(map)
81	        .map(([crop_name, total_qty]) => ({ crop_name, total_qty }))
82	        .sort((a, b) => b.total_qty - a.total_qty)
83	        .slice(0, 5)
84	    )
85	
86	    setLoading(false)
87	  }, [router])
88	
89	  useEffect(() => { loadDashboard() }, [loadDashboard])
90	
91	  // Auto-open the profile edit modal the first time an incomplete farmer lands here.
92	  useEffect(() => {
93	    if (!loading && farmer && !isProfileComplete(farmer)) {
94	      setShowProfileEdit(true)
95	    }
96	  }, [loading, farmer])
97	
98	  const handleLogout = () => {
99	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_id')
100	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_slug')
101	    router.replace('/farmer/login')
102	  }
103	
104	  if (loading) return <LoadingScreen />
105	  if (notFound) return <FarmerNotFound onLogout={handleLogout} />
106	
107	  const profileComplete = isProfileComplete(farmer)
108	  const displayName = farmer!.name?.trim() || 'Welcome'
109	
110	  return (
111	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
112	      {/* Header */}
113	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
114	        <div className="flex justify-end mb-2">
115	          <LanguageToggle />
116	        </div>
117	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
118	          <div>
119	            <p className="text-green-400 text-xs font-semibold mb-0.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
120	              Farmer Dashboard / రైతు డాష్‌బోర్డ్
121	            </p>
122	            <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{displayName}</h1>
123	            <p className="text-green-300 text-sm mt-0.5">
124	              {profileComplete
125	                ? `${farmer!.village}, ${farmer!.district}`
126	                : 'Complete your profile to get started / ప్రొఫైల్ పూర్తి చేయండి'}
127	            </p>
128	            <p className="text-green-500 text-xs mt-1">+91 {farmer!.phone}</p>
129	          </div>
130	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
131	            {profileComplete ? (
132	              <Link
133	                href={`/farmer/${farmer!.slug}`}
134	                className="bg-white text-green-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl"
135	              >
136	                View profile ↗
137	              </Link>
138	            ) : (
139	              <span className="bg-amber-400 text-amber-900 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
140	                Incomplete
141	              </span>
142	            )}
143	            <button
144	              onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
145	              className="text-white text-xs underline"
146	            >
147	              Edit profile
148	            </button>
149	            <button onClick={handleLogout} className="text-green-500 text-xs underline">
150	              Logout
151	            </button>
152	          </div>
153	        </div>
154	      </div>
155	
156	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
157	        {/* Complete profile banner */}
158	        {!profileComplete && (
159	          <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
160	            <span className="text-2xl flex-shrink-0">📝</span>
161	            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
162	              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
163	                Complete your profile
164	              </h3>
165	              <p className="text-amber-700 text-xs mt-0.5">
166	                ప్రొఫైల్ పూర్తి చేస్తే బయ్యర్లు మిమ్మల్ని కనుగొంటారు
167	              </p>
168	              <button
169	                onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
170	                className="mt-3 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm"
171	              >
172	                Fill details / వివరాలు నింపండి
173	              </button>
174	            </div>
175	          </div>
176	        )}
177	
178	        {/* Stat cards */}
179	        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
180	          {[
181	            { label: 'Active listings', sub: 'చురుకైన పంటలు', value: activeListings, color: 'border-green-200 bg-green-50', vcolor: 'text-green-800' },
182	            { label: 'Orders this week', sub: 'ఈ వారం ఆర్డర్లు', value: ordersCount, color: 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50', vcolor: 'text-blue-800' },
183	            { label: 'Avg rating', sub: 'సగటు రేటింగ్', value: farmer!.rating_avg ? `${Number(farmer!.rating_avg).toFixed(1)} ★` : '—', color: 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50', vcolor: 'text-amber-800' },
184	            { label: 'Total buyers', sub: 'మొత్తం కొనుగోలుదారులు', value: farmer!.buyer_count ?? 0, color: 'border-purple-200 bg-purple-50', vcolor: 'text-purple-800' },
185	          ].map((s) => (
186	            <div key={s.label} className={`${s.color} border rounded-2xl p-4`}>
187	              <div className={`text-3xl font-black ${s.vcolor}`}>{s.value}</div>
188	              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1 leading-tight">{s.label}</div>
189	              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{s.sub}</div>
190	            </div>
191	          ))}
192	        </div>
193	
194	        {/* Demand chart */}
195	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
196	          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
197	            Local demand / స్థానిక డిమాండ్
198	          </h2>
199	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mb-4">
200	            మీ ప్రాంతంలో వినియోగదారులు ఏమి కోరుతున్నారు
201	          </p>
202	
203	          {demandBars.length === 0 ? (
204	            <div className="text-center py-6">
205	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">No demand signals yet.</p>
206	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-1">Share your profile link to attract buyers.</p>
207	            </div>
208	          ) : (
209	            <div className="space-y-3">
210	              {demandBars.map((bar) => {
211	                const pct = Math.round((bar.total_qty / demandBars[0].total_qty) * 100)
212	                return (
213	                  <div key={bar.crop_name}>
214	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
215	                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">{bar.crop_name}</span>
216	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">{bar.total_qty} kg</span>
217	                    </div>
218	                    <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
219	                      <div
220	                        className="h-full bg-green-600 rounded-full"
221	                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
222	                      />
223	                    </div>
224	                  </div>
225	                )
226	              })}
227	            </div>
228	          )}
229	        </div>
230	
231	        {/* Add listing button */}
232	        {profileComplete ? (
233	          !showForm && (
234	            <button
235	              onClick={() => setShowForm(true)}
236	              className="w-full bg-white border-2 border-green-700 text-green-700 font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:bg-green-50"
237	            >
238	              <span className="text-xl leading-none">+</span>
239	              Add new produce / కొత్త పంట చేర్చండి
240	            </button>
241	          )
242	        ) : (
243	          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 font-semibold py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm text-center">
244	            Complete your profile before adding produce<br />
245	            <span className="text-xs">ముందుగా ప్రొఫైల్ పూర్తి చేయండి</span>
246	          </div>
247	        )}
248	
249	        {/* Listing form */}
250	        {showForm && profileComplete && (
251	          <ProduceListingForm
252	            farmerId={farmer!.id}
253	            farmerSlug={farmer!.slug}
254	            defaultMethod={farmer!.method}
255	            onClose={() => setShowForm(false)}
256	            onPublished={() => { setShowForm(false); loadDashboard() }}
257	          />
258	        )}
259	      </div>
260	
261	      {/* Edit profile modal */}
262	      {showProfileEdit && farmer && (
263	        <ProfileEditModal
264	          farmer={farmer}
265	          onClose={() => {
266	            // If profile still incomplete, don't allow close (keep banner as fallback)
267	            if (isProfileComplete(farmer)) setShowProfileEdit(false)
268	            else setShowProfileEdit(false) // allow dismiss; banner still shown
269	          }}
270	          onSaved={(updated) => {
271	            setFarmer(updated)
272	            setShowProfileEdit(false)
273	          }}
274	        />
275	      )}
276	    </main>
277	  )
278	}
279	
280	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */
281	function ProfileEditModal({
282	  farmer,
283	  onClose,
284	  onSaved,
285	}: {
286	  farmer: Farmer
287	  onClose: () => void
288	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
289	}) {
290	  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')
291	  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')
292	  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')
293	  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')
294	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
295	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
296	  )
297	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
298	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
299	
300	  const handleSave = async () => {
301	    if (!name.trim()) { setError('Your name is required / మీ పేరు అవసరం'); return }
302	    if (!village.trim()) { setError('Village is required / గ్రామం అవసరం'); return }
303	    setLoading(true)
304	    setError('')
305	
306	    // Build a unique slug from the name once the farmer actually has one.
307	    // Only regenerate slug if current slug still looks auto-generated (f-<digits>-xxxx).
308	    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')
309	    const baseSlug = name
310	      .toLowerCase()
311	      .trim()
312	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
313	      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
314	      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'
315	
316	    let newSlug = farmer.slug
317	    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {
318	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)
319	      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`
320	    }
321	
322	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
323	      name: name.trim(),
324	      village: village.trim(),
325	      district: district.trim(),
326	      method,
327	      slug: newSlug,
328	    }
329	    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)
330	
331	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
332	      .from('farmers')
333	      .update(payload)
334	      .eq('id', farmer.id)
335	      .select('*')
336	      .single()
337	
338	    setLoading(false)
339	
340	    if (err || !data) {
341	      setError(err?.message ?? 'Could not save. Please try again.')
342	      return
343	    }
344	
345	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)
346	    onSaved(data)
347	  }
348	
349	  return (
350	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
351	      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
352	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
353	          <div>
354	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">Your farmer profile</h3>
355	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">మీ రైతు ప్రొఫైల్</p>
356	          </div>
357	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
358	        </div>
359	
360	        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
361	          <Field
362	            label="Your name / మీ పేరు *"
363	            placeholder="Ramu Reddy"
364	            value={name}
365	            onChange={setName}
366	          />
367	          <Field
368	            label="Village / గ్రామం *"
369	            placeholder="Tadepalligudem"
370	            value={village}
371	            onChange={setVillage}
372	          />
373	          <Field
374	            label="District / జిల్లా"
375	            placeholder="West Godavari"
376	            value={district}
377	            onChange={setDistrict}
378	          />
379	
380	          <div>
381	            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
382	              Farming method / వ్యవసాయ పద్ధతి
383	            </label>
384	            <select
385	              value={method}
386	              onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
387	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
388	            >
389	              <option value="natural">🌱 Natural / సహజం</option>
390	              <option value="organic">🍃 Organic / సేంద్రీయ</option>
391	              <option value="low_chemical">⚡ Low chemical / తక్కువ రసాయన</option>
392	              <option value="chemical">Chemical / రసాయన</option>
393	            </select>
394	          </div>
395	
396	          <Field
397	            label="Farming since (year) / ఏ సంవత్సరం నుండి"
398	            placeholder="e.g. 2005"
399	            value={sinceYear}
400	            onChange={setSinceYear}
401	            type="number"
402	          />
403	
404	          {error && (
405	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
406	          )}
407	
408	          <button
409	            onClick={handleSave}
410	            disabled={loading || !name.trim() || !village.trim()}
411	            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800"
412	          >
413	            {loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save profile / ప్రొఫైల్ సేవ్'}
414	          </button>
415	        </div>
416	      </div>
417	    </div>
418	  )
419	}
420	
421	function Field({
422	  label,
423	  placeholder,
424	  value,
425	  onChange,
426	  type = 'text',
427	}: {
428	  label: string
429	  placeholder?: string
430	  value: string
431	  onChange: (v: string) => void
432	  type?: string
433	}) {
434	  return (
435	    <div>
436	      <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
437	        {label}
438	      </label>
439	      <input
440	        type={type}
441	        placeholder={placeholder}
442	        value={value}
443	        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
444	        className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
445	      />
446	    </div>
447	  )
448	}
449	
450	/* ─── Produce listing form ──────────────────────────────────── */
451	function ProduceListingForm({
452	  farmerId,
453	  farmerSlug,
454	  defaultMethod,
455	  onClose,
456	  onPublished,
457	}: {
458	  farmerId: string
459	  farmerSlug: string
460	  defaultMethod: string
461	  onClose: () => void
462	  onPublished: () => void
463	}) {
464	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
465	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
466	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')
467	  const [qty, setQty] = useState('')
468	  const [period, setPeriod] = useState('')
469	  const [farmingMethod, setFarmingMethod] = useState(defaultMethod || 'natural')
470	  const [price1, setPrice1] = useState('')
471	  const [price1Qty, setPrice1Qty] = useState('5')
472	  const [price2, setPrice2] = useState('')
473	  const [price2Qty, setPrice2Qty] = useState('20')
474	  const [price3, setPrice3] = useState('')
475	  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
476	  const [brix, setBrix] = useState('')
477	  const [soc, setSoc] = useState('')
478	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
479	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
480	  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(false)
481	  const [published, setPublished] = useState(false)
482	  const [publishedSlug, setPublishedSlug] = useState('')
483	
484	  const previewData: PreviewData = {
485	    name: name || 'Produce name',
486	    variety: variety || '',
487	    emoji,
488	    price: price1 || '—',
489	    method: farmingMethod,
490	    stock: qty || '—',
491	  }
492	
493	  const handlePublish = async () => {
494	    if (!name.trim()) { setError('Produce name is required'); return }
495	    setLoading(true)
496	    setError('')
497	
498	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
499	      farmer_id: farmerId,
500	      name: name.trim(),
501	      emoji,
502	      status: 'available',
503	      method: farmingMethod,
504	    }
505	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
506	    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)
507	    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()
508	    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)
509	    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)
510	    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }
511	    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }
512	    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }
513	
514	    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload)
515	    setLoading(false)
516	
517	    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
518	
519	    setPublished(true)
520	    setPublishedSlug(farmerSlug)
521	    setTimeout(() => onPublished(), 2500)
522	  }
523	
524	  if (published) {
525	    return (
526	      <div className="bg-green-50 border-2 border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center space-y-3">
527	        <div className="text-4xl">✅</div>
528	        <p className="font-extrabold text-green-800 text-lg">Published!</p>
529	        <p className="text-green-700 text-sm">Your listing is now live on the consumer page.</p>
530	        <Link
531	          href={`/farmer/${publishedSlug}`}
532	          className="inline-block bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
533	        >
534	          View your profile ↗
535	        </Link>
536	      </div>
537	    )
538	  }
539	
540	  return (
541	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
542	      {/* Form header */}
543	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
544	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">New produce listing</h3>
545	        <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
546	      </div>
547	
548	      <div className="p-4 space-y-5">
549	        {/* Emoji picker */}
550	        <div>
551	          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 mb-2">Pick an icon</p>
552	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
553	            {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
554	              <button
555	                key={e}
556	                onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
557	                className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
558	                  emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'
559	                }`}
560	              >
561	                {e}
562	              </button>
563	            ))}
564	          </div>
565	        </div>
566	
567	        {/* Name + variety */}
568	        <div className="space-y-2">
569	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Produce details</label>
570	          <input
571	            type="text"
572	            placeholder="Produce name * (e.g. Tomato / టమాటా)"
573	            value={name}
574	            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
575	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
576	          />
577	          <input
578	            type="text"
579	            placeholder="Variety (e.g. Naati heirloom)"
580	            value={variety}
581	            onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
582	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
583	          />
584	        </div>
585	
586	        {/* Qty + period */}
587	        <div className="space-y-2">
588	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Availability</label>
589	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
590	            <input
591	              type="number"
592	              placeholder="Quantity (kg)"
593	              value={qty}
594	              onChange={(e) => setQty(e.target.value)}
595	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
596	            />
597	            <input
598	              type="text"
599	              placeholder="Period (e.g. Apr–May)"
600	              value={period}
601	              onChange={(e) => setPeriod(e.target.value)}
602	              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
603	            />
604	          </div>
605	        </div>
606	
607	        {/* Farming method */}
608	        <div className="space-y-2">
609	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Farming method</label>
610	          <select
611	            value={farmingMethod}
612	            onChange={(e) => setFarmingMethod(e.target.value)}
613	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
614	          >
615	            <option value="natural">🌱 Natural / సహజం</option>
616	            <option value="organic">🍃 Organic / సేంద్రీయ</option>
617	            <option value="low_chemical">⚡ Low chemical</option>
618	            <option value="chemical">Chemical</option>
619	          </select>
620	        </div>
621	
622	        {/* Delivery method */}
623	        <div className="space-y-2">
624	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Delivery method</label>
625	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
626	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-2 border-green-600 bg-green-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-3">
627	              <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-green-600 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
628	                <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-white rounded-full" />
629	              </div>
630	              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-green-800">Pickup only</span>
631	            </div>
632	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed">
633	              <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-300 flex-shrink-0" />
634	              <div>
635	                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-400">I will courier</span>
636	                <span className="block text-[10px] text-gray-400">Coming soon</span>
637	              </div>
638	            </div>
639	          </div>
640	        </div>
641	
642	        {/* Pricing tiers */}
643	        <div className="space-y-2">
644	          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Pricing tiers</label>
645	          <div className="space-y-2">

803	            </div>
804	            <div className="p-3">
805	              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">{data.name}</h3>
806	              {data.variety && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{data.variety}</p>}
807	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">

834	          </button>
835	        </div>
836	      </div>
837	    </div>

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
3	import { Suspense } from 'react'
4	import TopNav from '@/components/farmer/TopNav'
5	import FarmCover from '@/components/farmer/FarmCover'
6	import TrustStrip from '@/components/farmer/TrustStrip'
7	import TabSection from '@/components/farmer/TabSection'
8	import StickyBottomBar from '@/components/farmer/StickyBottomBar'
9	
10	export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
11	  const { slug } = await params
12	
13	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
14	    .from('farmers')
15	    .select('id, name, village, district, story_quote')
16	    .eq('slug', slug)
17	    .single()
18	
19	  if (!farmer) return { title: 'Farmer not found' }
20	
21	  const { data: firstPhoto } = await supabase
22	    .from('media')
23	    .select('url')
24	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
25	    .eq('type', 'photo')
26	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
27	    .limit(1)
28	    .single()
29	
30	  const title = `${farmer.name} — Natural farmer in ${farmer.village}`
31	  const description =
32	    farmer.story_quote ??
33	    `Buy natural produce directly from ${farmer.name}, ${farmer.village}, ${farmer.district}. No middlemen. No chemicals.`
34	
35	  return {
36	    title: `${farmer.name}'s Farm — YourFamilyFarmer`,
37	    description,
38	    openGraph: {
39	      title,
40	      description,
41	      siteName: 'YourFamilyFarmer',
42	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [{ url: firstPhoto.url, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: `${farmer.name}'s farm` }] } : {}),
43	    },
44	    twitter: {
45	      card: 'summary_large_image',
46	      title,
47	      description,
48	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [firstPhoto.url] } : {}),
49	    },
50	  }
51	}
52	
53	export default async function FarmerPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
54	  const { slug } = await params
55	
56	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
57	    .from('farmers')
58	    .select('*')
59	    .eq('slug', slug)
60	    .eq('active', true)
61	    .single()
62	
63	  if (!farmer) notFound()
64	
65	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
66	    .from('produce_listings')
67	    .select('*')
68	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
69	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
70	
71	  const { data: reviews } = await supabase
72	    .from('reviews')
73	    .select('*')
74	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
75	    .eq('approved', true)
76	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
77	
78	  const { data: media } = await supabase
79	    .from('media')
80	    .select('*')
81	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
82	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
83	
84	  return (
85	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24">
86	      <TopNav regionSlug={farmer.region_slug} />
87	      <FarmCover farmer={farmer} />
88	      <TrustStrip farmer={farmer} produceCount={produce?.length ?? 0} />
89	      <Suspense fallback={null}>
90	        <TabSection
91	          farmer={farmer}
92	          produce={produce ?? []}
93	          reviews={reviews ?? []}
94	          media={media ?? []}
95	        />
96	      </Suspense>
97	      <StickyBottomBar farmer={farmer} />
98	    </main>
99	  )
100	}
101

3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
5	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
6	
7	type Produce = {
8	  id: string
9	  name: string
10	  variety?: string
11	  emoji?: string
12	  method?: string
13	  status: string
14	  price_tier_1_qty?: number
15	  price_tier_1_price?: number
16	  price_tier_2_qty?: number
17	  price_tier_2_price?: number
18	  price_tier_3_qty?: number
19	  price_tier_3_price?: number
20	  stock_qty?: number

22	  pesticide_result?: string
23	  shelf_life_days?: number
24	  available_to?: string
25	}
26	
27	type Farmer = {
28	  id?: string
29	  name?: string
30	  phone?: string
31	}
32	
33	const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾']
34	const STATUS_OPTIONS = ['available', 'coming_soon']
35	
36	export default function ProduceTab({
37	  farmer,
38	  produce,
39	  isEditMode = false,
40	}: {
41	  farmer: Record<string, unknown>
42	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
43	  isEditMode?: boolean
44	}) {
45	  const { tx } = useLang()
46	  const f = farmer as Farmer
47	  const [listings, setListings] = useState<Produce[]>(produce as Produce[])
48	  const available = listings.filter((p) => p.status === 'available')
49	  const comingSoon = listings.filter((p) => p.status === 'coming_soon')
50	
51	  const buildWhatsAppLink = (item: Produce) => {
52	    const message = tx.orderMessage
53	      .replace('{name}', f.name ?? '')
54	      .replace('{produce}', `${item.name}${item.variety ? ` (${item.variety})` : ''}`)
55	    return `https://wa.me/${f.phone?.replace(/\D/g, '')}?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`
56	  }
57	
58	  const handleProduceAdded = (newItem: Produce) => {
59	    setListings((prev) => [newItem, ...prev])
60	  }
61	
62	  return (
63	    <div className="space-y-6">
64	      {available.length > 0 && (
65	        <div>
66	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
67	            <span className="bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
68	              {tx.availableNow}
69	            </span>
70	          </div>
71	          <div className="space-y-3">
72	            {available.map((item) => (
73	              <ProduceCard key={item.id} item={item} whatsappLink={buildWhatsAppLink(item)} />
74	            ))}
75	          </div>
76	        </div>
77	      )}
78	
79	      {comingSoon.length > 0 && (
80	        <div>
81	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
82	            <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
83	              {tx.comingSoon}
84	            </span>
85	          </div>
86	          <div className="space-y-3">
87	            {comingSoon.map((item) => (
88	              <ComingSoonCard key={item.id} item={item} farmerId={item.id} />
89	            ))}
90	          </div>
91	        </div>
92	      )}
93	
94	      {listings.length === 0 && (
95	        <div className="text-center py-6 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noProduceListed}</div>
96	      )}
97	
98	      {isEditMode && f.id && (
99	        <AddProduceForm farmerId={f.id} onAdded={handleProduceAdded} />
100	      )}
101	    </div>
102	  )
103	}
104	
105	function AddProduceForm({ farmerId, onAdded }: { farmerId: string; onAdded: (item: Produce) => void }) {
106	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
107	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
108	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
109	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')
110	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'available' | 'coming_soon'>('available')
111	  const [price, setPrice] = useState('')
112	  const [stock, setStock] = useState('')
113	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
114	  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
115	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
116	
117	  const reset = () => {
118	    setName(''); setVariety(''); setEmoji('🌿'); setStatus('available')
119	    setPrice(''); setStock(''); setSuccess(false); setError('')
120	  }
121	
122	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
123	    if (!name.trim()) return
124	    setLoading(true)
125	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
126	      farmer_id: farmerId,
127	      name: name.trim(),
128	      emoji,
129	      status,
130	      method: 'Natural',
131	    }
132	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
133	    if (price) payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price)
134	    if (stock) payload.stock_qty = Number(stock)
135	
136	    const { data, error: insertError } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload).select().single()
137	    setLoading(false)
138	    if (!insertError && data) {
139	      onAdded(data as Produce)
140	      setSuccess(true)
141	      setTimeout(() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }, 1500)
142	    } else {
143	      setError(insertError?.message ?? 'Failed to add produce. Check Supabase RLS policies.')
144	    }
145	  }
146	
147	  if (!open) {
148	    return (
149	      <button
150	        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
151	        className="w-full border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 text-green-700 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-50 transition-colors"
152	      >
153	        <span className="text-lg">+</span> Add your produce
154	      </button>
155	    )
156	  }
157	
158	  return (
159	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-4 space-y-4">
160	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
161	        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">Add produce</h3>
162	        <button onClick={() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }} className="text-gray-400 text-lg leading-none">×</button>
163	      </div>
164	
165	      {/* Emoji picker */}
166	      <div>
167	        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">Pick an icon</p>
168	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
169	          {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
170	            <button
171	              key={e}
172	              onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
173	              className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'}`}
174	            >
175	              {e}
176	            </button>
177	          ))}
178	        </div>
179	      </div>
180	
181	      {/* Name + variety */}
182	      <div className="space-y-2">
183	        <input
184	          type="text"
185	          placeholder="Produce name (e.g. Papaya)"
186	          value={name}
187	          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
188	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
189	        />
190	        <input
191	          type="text"
192	          placeholder="Variety (optional, e.g. Red Lady)"
193	          value={variety}
194	          onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
195	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
196	        />
197	      </div>
198	
199	      {/* Price + stock */}
200	      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
201	        <input
202	          type="number"
203	          placeholder="Price / kg (₹)"
204	          value={price}
205	          onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)}
206	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
207	        />
208	        <input
209	          type="number"
210	          placeholder="Stock (kg)"
211	          value={stock}
212	          onChange={(e) => setStock(e.target.value)}
213	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
214	        />
215	      </div>
216	
217	      {/* Status */}
218	      <div className="flex gap-2">
219	        {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((s) => (
220	          <button
221	            key={s}
222	            onClick={() => setStatus(s as 'available' | 'coming_soon')}
223	            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
224	              status === s
225	                ? s === 'available' ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
226	                : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'
227	            }`}
228	          >
229	            {s === 'available' ? 'Available now' : 'Coming soon'}
230	          </button>
231	        ))}
232	      </div>
233	
234	      {error && (
235	        <p className="text-center text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
236	      )}
237	
238	      {success ? (
239	        <p className="text-center text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold py-1">✓ Added successfully!</p>
240	      ) : (
241	        <button
242	          onClick={handleSubmit}
243	          disabled={loading || !name.trim()}
244	          className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50"
245	        >
246	          {loading ? 'Adding...' : 'Add produce'}
247	        </button>
248	      )}
249	    </div>
250	  )
251	}
252	
253	function ProduceCard({ item, whatsappLink }: { item: Produce; whatsappLink: string }) {
254	  const { tx } = useLang()
255	  const bgColors: Record<string, string> = {
256	    '🍅': 'bg-red-100', '🥬': 'bg-green-100', '🥭': 'bg-orange-100',

259	  }

261	  const bg = bgColors[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-100'
262	
263	  return (
264	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
265	      <div className="flex gap-3 p-3">

274	            </div>

282	            )}
283	            {item.pesticide_result && (

285	            )}
286	            {item.brix && (
287	              <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">BRIX {item.brix}</span>
288	            )}
289	          </div>
290	        </div>
291	      </div>
292	
293	      {item.price_tier_1_price && (
294	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-50">
295	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-700">
296	            {item.price_tier_1_price && (

298	            )}

300	              <span>{item.price_tier_2_qty}–{item.price_tier_3_qty ? item.price_tier_3_qty - 1 : '+'} kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_2_price}</strong></span>
301	            )}
302	            {item.price_tier_3_qty && item.price_tier_3_price && (
303	              <span>{item.price_tier_3_qty}+ kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_3_price}</strong></span>
304	            )}
305	          </div>
306	        </div>
307	      )}
308	
309	      <div className="px-3 pb-3 pt-2">
310	        <a
311	          href={whatsappLink}
312	          target="_blank"
313	          rel="noopener noreferrer"
314	          className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full text-center bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-base"
315	        >
316	          <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor">

326	function ComingSoonCard({ item, farmerId }: { item: Produce; farmerId: string }) {
327	  const { tx } = useLang()
328	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
329	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
330	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
331	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
332	
333	  const handleNotify = async () => {
334	    if (!name || !phone) return
335	    setLoading(true)
336	    await supabase.from('notify_requests').insert({
337	      farmer_id: farmerId,
338	      produce_name: item.name,
339	      requester_name: name,
340	      requester_phone: phone,
341	    })
342	    setSubmitted(true)
343	    setLoading(false)
344	  }
345	
346	  return (
347	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 p-4">
348	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
349	        <div>
350	          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{item.emoji ?? '🌱'} {item.name}</h3>
351	          {item.available_to && (
352	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
353	              {tx.expected} {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
354	            </p>
355	          )}
356	        </div>
357	        <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
358	          {tx.comingSoon}
359	        </span>
360	      </div>
361	
362	      {submitted ? (
363	        <p className="text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold text-center py-2">{tx.notifySuccess}</p>
364	      ) : (
365	        <div className="space-y-2 mt-3">
366	          <input type="text" placeholder={tx.yourName} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
367	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
368	          <input type="tel" placeholder={tx.yourPhone} value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
369	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
370	          <button onClick={handleNotify} disabled={loading}
371	            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
372	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.notifyMe}
373	          </button>
374	        </div>
375	      )}
376	    </div>
377	  )
378	}

1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import Link from 'next/link'
7	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
8	
9	export default function FarmerLoginPage() {
10	  const router = useRouter()
11	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
12	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
13	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
14	
15	  const handleLogin = async () => {
16	    const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)
17	    if (digits.length < 10) return
18	    setLoading(true)
19	    setError('')
20	
21	    // 1. Try all common phone formats stored in DB
22	    const { data: farmers } = await supabase
23	      .from('farmers')
24	      .select('id, name, slug, phone')
25	      .or([
26	        `phone.eq.${digits}`,
27	        `phone.eq.0${digits}`,
28	        `phone.eq.+91${digits}`,
29	        `phone.eq.91${digits}`,
30	      ].join(','))
31	      .limit(1)
32	
33	    let farmer = farmers?.[0]
34	
35	    // 2. If not found, auto-create a minimal farmer record.
36	    if (!farmer) {
37	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)
38	      const slug = `f-${digits}-${rand}`
39	
40	      const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase
41	        .from('farmers')
42	        .insert({
43	          phone: digits,
44	          slug,
45	          name: '',
46	          village: '',
47	          district: '',
48	          method: 'natural',
49	          region_slug: 'tadepalligudem',
50	          active: true,
51	        })
52	        .select('id, name, slug, phone')
53	        .single()
54	
55	      if (insertErr || !created) {
56	        setLoading(false)
57	        setError(
58	          insertErr?.message ??
59	            'Could not create your farmer profile. Please try again.',
60	        )
61	        return
62	      }
63	      farmer = created
64	    }
65	
66	    setLoading(false)
67	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_id', farmer.id)
68	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', farmer.slug)
69	    router.replace('/farmer/dashboard')
70	  }
71	
72	  return (
73	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4">
74	      <div className="absolute top-4 right-4">
75	        <LanguageToggle />
76	      </div>
77	      <div className="w-full max-w-sm">
78	        <div className="text-center mb-8">
79	          <Link href="/consumer" className="inline-flex flex-col items-center">
80	            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-700 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
81	              <span className="text-white font-black text-lg">YFF</span>
82	            </div>
83	          </Link>
84	          <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Farmer Login</h1>
85	          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-1">రైతు లాగిన్</p>
86	        </div>
87	
88	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-6 space-y-4">
89	          <div>
90	            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 block mb-2">
91	              WhatsApp Number / వాట్సాప్ నంబర్
92	            </label>
93	            <div className="flex gap-2">
94	              <span className="flex items-center px-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
95	                +91
96	              </span>
97	              <input
98	                type="tel"
99	                inputMode="numeric"
100	                placeholder="9876543210"
101	                value={phone}
102	                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
103	                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleLogin()}
104	                className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
105	                maxLength={10}
106	              />
107	            </div>
108	          </div>
109	
110	          {error && (
111	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
112	          )}
113	
114	          <button
115	            onClick={handleLogin}
116	            disabled={loading || phone.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10}
117	            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800 transition-colors"
118	          >
119	            {loading ? 'Please wait...' : 'Continue / కొనసాగండి'}
120	          </button>
121	
122	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 text-center leading-snug">
123	            New here? Just enter your number and continue — we&apos;ll set up your profile.<br />
124	            కొత్తవారా? మీ నంబర్ నమోదు చేసి కొనసాగండి.
125	          </p>
126	        </div>
127	
128	        <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400 mt-6">
129	          <Link href="/consumer" className="text-green-700 underline">Browse produce instead</Link>
130	        </p>
131	      </div>
132	    </main>
133	  )
134	}
135

5	type Farmer = {

11	export default function TrustStrip({
12	  farmer,
13	  produceCount,
14	}: {
15	  farmer: Farmer
16	  produceCount: number
17	}) {
18	  const { tx } = useLang()
19	  const yearsfarming = new Date().getFullYear() - farmer.farming_since_year
20	
21	  const stats = [
22	    { label: tx.yearsFarming, value: yearsfarming.toString() },

26	    { label: tx.produceNow, value: `${produceCount}` },
27	  ]
28	
29	  return (
30	    <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-100">
31	      <div className="grid grid-cols-5 divide-x divide-gray-100">

36	              {stat.label}
37	            </span>
38	          </div>
39	        ))}
40	      </div>
41	    </div>
42	  )
43	}

8	  name: string
9	  variety?: string
10	  emoji?: string
11	  pricePerKg?: number
12	  farmerId: string
13	  farmerName: string
14	  farmerPhone: string
15	  farmerVillage: string
16	  farmerSlug: string
17	}
18	
19	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
20	
21	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
22	
23	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
24	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
25	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
26	
27	const readCart = (): CartState => {
28	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
29	  try {
30	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
31	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
32	  } catch {
33	    return {}
34	  }
35	}
36	
37	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
38	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
39	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
40	}
41	
42	export function useCart() {
43	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
44	
45	  useEffect(() => {
46	    setCart(readCart())
47	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
48	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
49	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
50	    return () => {
51	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
52	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
53	    }
54	  }, [])
55	
56	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
57	    const next = { ...readCart() }
58	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
59	    next[item.listingId] = {
60	      ...item,
61	      qty: Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty),
62	    }
63	    writeCart(next)
64	  }, [])
65	
66	  const setQty = useCallback((listingId: string, qty: number) => {
67	    const next = { ...readCart() }
68	    if (qty <= 0) delete next[listingId]
69	    else if (next[listingId]) next[listingId] = { ...next[listingId], qty }
70	    writeCart(next)
71	  }, [])
72	
73	  const removeItem = useCallback((listingId: string) => {
74	    const next = { ...readCart() }
75	    delete next[listingId]
76	    writeCart(next)
77	  }, [])
78	
79	  const clear = useCallback(() => writeCart({}), [])
80	
81	  const items = Object.values(cart)
82	  const count = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.qty, 0)
83	
84	  return { cart, items, count, addItem, setQty, removeItem, clear }
85	}
86	
87	export function useConsumerInfo() {
88	  const [info, setInfo] = useState<ConsumerInfo>({ name: '', phone: '' })
89	
90	  useEffect(() => {
91	    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
92	    try {
93	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CONSUMER_KEY)
94	      if (raw) setInfo(JSON.parse(raw))
95	    } catch { /* ignore */ }
96	  }, [])
97	
98	  const save = useCallback((next: ConsumerInfo) => {
99	    setInfo(next)
100	    localStorage.setItem(CONSUMER_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
101	  }, [])
102	
103	  return { info, save }
104	}
105	
106	/* ─── Cart FAB + Sheet ───────────────────────────────── */
107	export function CartFab() {
108	  const { items, count } = useCart()
109	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
110	
111	  if (count === 0 && !open) return null
112	
113	  return (
114	    <>
115	      {!open && (
116	        <button
117	          onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
118	          className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-40 bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white rounded-full shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-2 pl-4 pr-5 py-3.5"
119	          aria-label="View cart"
120	        >
121	          <CartIcon />
122	          <span className="font-extrabold text-sm">
123	            {count} {count === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} / బుట్ట
124	          </span>
125	        </button>
126	      )}
127	      {open && (
128	        <CartSheet items={items} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
129	      )}
130	    </>
131	  )
132	}
133	
134	/* ─── Cart sheet ─────────────────────────────────────── */
135	function CartSheet({
136	  items,
137	  onClose,
138	}: {
139	  items: CartItem[]
140	  onClose: () => void
141	}) {
142	  const { setQty, removeItem, clear } = useCart()
143	  const { info, save: saveInfo } = useConsumerInfo()
144	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
145	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
146	  const [sentFarmers, setSentFarmers] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})
147	
148	  useEffect(() => {
149	    setName(info.name)
150	    setPhone(info.phone)
151	  }, [info])
152	
153	  // Group items by farmer
154	  const byFarmer = items.reduce<Record<string, CartItem[]>>((acc, item) => {
155	    (acc[item.farmerId] = acc[item.farmerId] ?? []).push(item)
156	    return acc
157	  }, {})
158	  const farmerGroups = Object.values(byFarmer)
159	
160	  const detailsMissing = !name.trim() || phone.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10
161	
162	  const handleOrderFarmer = (group: CartItem[]) => {
163	    if (detailsMissing) return
164	    saveInfo({ name: name.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })
165	
166	    const f = group[0]
167	    const lines = group.map((it) => {
168	      const price = it.pricePerKg ? ` @ ₹${it.pricePerKg}/kg` : ''
169	      return `• ${it.emoji ?? '🌿'} ${it.name}${it.variety ? ` (${it.variety})` : ''} — ${it.qty} kg${price}`
170	    })
171	
172	    const msg =
173	      `Hello ${f.farmerName} anna! 🙏\n` +
174	      `I saw your produce on YourFamilyFarmer and would like to order:\n\n` +
175	      lines.join('\n') +
176	      `\n\n*Pickup from your farm* (${f.farmerVillage})\n\n` +
177	      `My name: ${name.trim()}\n` +
178	      `My WhatsApp: +91 ${phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)}\n\n` +
179	      `Please share a good pickup time. Thank you!\n` +
180	      `ధన్యవాదాలు 🌱`
181	
182	    const digits = f.farmerPhone.replace(/\D/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '')
183	    const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits
184	    const waUrl = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`
185	
186	    window.open(waUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')
187	    setSentFarmers((s) => ({ ...s, [f.farmerId]: true }))
188	  }
189	
190	  return (
191	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
192	      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col">
193	        {/* Header */}
194	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 rounded-t-3xl">
195	          <div>
196	            <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg">Your cart / మీ బుట్ట</h2>
197	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
198	              Pickup from farm only / పొలం నుండి పికప్ మాత్రమే
199	            </p>
200	          </div>
201	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
202	        </div>
203	
204	        {/* Body */}
205	        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-5">
206	          {items.length === 0 ? (
207	            <div className="text-center py-14 text-gray-400">
208	              <div className="text-5xl mb-3">🛒</div>
209	              <p className="font-semibold">Your cart is empty</p>
210	              <p className="text-sm mt-1">బుట్ట ఖాళీగా ఉంది</p>
211	            </div>
212	          ) : (
213	            <>
214	              {/* Consumer details */}
215	              <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
216	                <div>
217	                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1">
218	                    Your name / మీ పేరు
219	                  </label>
220	                  <input
221	                    type="text"
222	                    value={name}
223	                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
224	                    placeholder="Ramya"
225	                    className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
226	                  />
227	                </div>
228	                <div>
229	                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1">
230	                    Your WhatsApp number / వాట్సాప్ నంబర్
231	                  </label>
232	                  <div className="flex gap-2">
233	                    <span className="flex items-center px-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium">
234	                      +91
235	                    </span>
236	                    <input
237	                      type="tel"
238	                      inputMode="numeric"
239	                      value={phone}
240	                      onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
241	                      placeholder="9876543210"
242	                      maxLength={10}
243	                      className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
244	                    />
245	                  </div>
246	                </div>
247	                <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">
248	                  The farmer needs this to confirm pickup time.<br />
249	                  పికప్ సమయం కోసం రైతుకు ఇది అవసరం.
250	                </p>
251	              </div>
252	
253	              {/* Farmer groups */}
254	              {farmerGroups.map((group) => {
255	                const f = group[0]
256	                const total = group.reduce(
257	                  (s, it) => s + (it.pricePerKg ?? 0) * it.qty,
258	                  0,
259	                )
260	                const sent = sentFarmers[f.farmerId]
261	                return (
262	                  <div
263	                    key={f.farmerId}
264	                    className="border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden"
265	                  >
266	                    <div className="bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 border-b border-green-100 flex items-center justify-between">
267	                      <div className="min-w-0">
268	                        <p className="font-extrabold text-green-900 truncate">
269	                          🧑‍🌾 {f.farmerName}
270	                        </p>
271	                        <p className="text-xs text-green-700">📍 {f.farmerVillage}</p>
272	                      </div>
273	                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap">
274	                        Pickup
275	                      </span>
276	                    </div>
277	
278	                    <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
279	                      {group.map((it) => (
280	                        <div key={it.listingId} className="flex items-center gap-3">
281	                          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center text-xl flex-shrink-0">
282	                            {it.emoji ?? '🌿'}
283	                          </div>
284	                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
285	                            <p className="font-semibold text-sm text-gray-900 truncate">
286	                              {it.name}
287	                            </p>
288	                            {it.pricePerKg && (
289	                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">₹{it.pricePerKg}/kg</p>
290	                            )}
291	                          </div>
292	                          <QtyStepper
293	                            qty={it.qty}
294	                            onDec={() => setQty(it.listingId, it.qty - 1)}
295	                            onInc={() => setQty(it.listingId, it.qty + 1)}
296	                          />
297	                          <button
298	                            onClick={() => removeItem(it.listingId)}
299	                            className="text-gray-300 text-xl px-1"
300	                            aria-label="Remove"
301	                          >
302	                            ×
303	                          </button>
304	                        </div>
305	                      ))}
306	
307	                      {total > 0 && (
308	                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 mt-2">
309	                          <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Estimated total</span>
310	                          <span className="font-extrabold text-gray-900">
311	                            ₹{total}
312	                          </span>
313	                        </div>
314	                      )}
315	
316	                      <button
317	                        onClick={() => handleOrderFarmer(group)}
318	                        disabled={detailsMissing}
319	                        className={`mt-1 w-full font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
320	                          sent
321	                            ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
322	                            : detailsMissing
323	                              ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-500'
324	                              : 'bg-green-700 text-white active:bg-green-800'
325	                        }`}
326	                      >
327	                        {sent ? (
328	                          <>✓ Order sent — tap to resend</>
329	                        ) : (
330	                          <>
331	                            <WhatsAppIcon />
332	                            Send to {f.farmerName.split(' ')[0] || 'farmer'} / వాట్సాప్
333	                          </>
334	                        )}
335	                      </button>
336	                    </div>
337	                  </div>
338	                )
339	              })}
340	
341	              {farmerGroups.length > 1 && (
342	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 text-center px-4 leading-snug">
343	                  One WhatsApp chat opens per farmer, since each farm handles its own pickup.<br />
344	                  ప్రతి రైతుకు ప్రత్యేక వాట్సాప్ చాట్ తెరుచుకుంటుంది.
345	                </p>
346	              )}
347	
348	              <button
349	                onClick={clear}
350	                className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 underline pt-2"
351	              >
352	                Clear cart / బుట్ట ఖాళీ చేయండి
353	              </button>
354	            </>
355	          )}
356	        </div>
357	      </div>
358	    </div>
359	  )
360	}
361	
362	function QtyStepper({
363	  qty,
364	  onDec,
365	  onInc,
366	}: {
367	  qty: number
368	  onDec: () => void
369	  onInc: () => void
370	}) {
371	  return (
372	    <div className="flex items-center border border-gray-200 rounded-full">
373	      <button
374	        onClick={onDec}
375	        className="w-8 h-8 text-lg text-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-100 rounded-l-full"
376	        aria-label="Decrease"
377	      >
378	        −
379	      </button>
380	      <span className="w-10 text-center font-bold text-sm text-gray-900">
381	        {qty}
382	      </span>
383	      <button
384	        onClick={onInc}
385	        className="w-8 h-8 text-lg text-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-100 rounded-r-full"
386	        aria-label="Increase"
387	      >
388	        +
389	      </button>
390	    </div>
391	  )
392	}
393

395	  return (
396	    <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
397	      <circle cx="9" cy="21" r="1" />
398	      <circle cx="20" cy="21" r="1" />
399	      <path d="M1 1h4l2.68 13.39a2 2 0 0 0 2 1.61h9.72a2 2 0 0 0 2-1.61L23 6H6" />
400	    </svg>
401	  )
402	}
403

405	  return (
406	    <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor">

1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
5	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
6	  const status = searchParams.get('status') ?? 'available'
7	
8	  const { data: produce, error } = await supabase
9	    .from('produce_listings')
10	    .select('*')
11	    .eq('status', status)
12	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
13	
14	  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
15	  if (!produce?.length) return NextResponse.json([])
16	
17	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(produce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
18	
19	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
20	    .from('farmers')
21	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug')
22	    .in('id', farmerIds)
23	    .eq('active', true)
24	
25	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
26	
27	  const result = produce
28	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
29	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
30	
31	  return NextResponse.json(result)
32	}
33
