```
1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	const CATEGORY_KEYWORDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
5	  vegetables: [
6	    'tomato', 'capsicum', 'brinjal', 'eggplant', 'bean', 'pea', 'pumpkin',
7	    'gourd', 'onion', 'potato', 'garlic', 'carrot', 'radish', 'cucumber',
8	    'okra', 'ladyfinger', 'drumstick', 'ridge gourd', 'bottle gourd', 'chilli',
9	    'pepper', 'bitter gourd', 'cluster bean', 'snake gourd',
10	  ],
11	  fruits: [
12	    'mango', 'banana', 'papaya', 'guava', 'pomegranate', 'orange', 'lime',
13	    'lemon', 'coconut', 'tamarind', 'jackfruit', 'sapota', 'fig', 'grapes',
14	    'watermelon', 'muskmelon', 'pineapple', 'custard apple',
15	  ],
16	  grains: [
17	    'rice', 'wheat', 'jowar', 'bajra', 'ragi', 'maize', 'corn', 'dal',
18	    'lentil', 'chickpea', 'groundnut', 'sesame', 'turmeric', 'ginger',
19	    'pulses', 'toor', 'moong', 'urad', 'chana',
20	  ],
21	  leafy: [
22	    'spinach', 'methi', 'fenugreek', 'coriander', 'mint', 'curry',
23	    'amaranth', 'sorrel', 'moringa', 'drumstick leaves', 'palak',
24	  ],
25	}
26	
27	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
28	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
29	  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
30	  const method = searchParams.get('method') ?? ''
31	  const category = searchParams.get('category') ?? ''
32	
33	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
34	    .from('produce_listings')
35	    .select('*')
36	    .eq('status', 'available')
37	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
38	
39	  let filtered = produce ?? []
40	
41	  if (q) {
42	    const query = q.toLowerCase()
43	    filtered = filtered.filter(
44	      (p) =>
45	        p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
46	        p.variety?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
47	        p.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
48	    )
49	  }
50	
51	  if (method && method !== 'all') {
52	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
53	      p.method?.toLowerCase().includes(method.toLowerCase())
54	    )
55	  }
56	
57	  if (category && category !== 'all' && CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]) {
58	    const keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]
59	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
60	      keywords.some((kw) => p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
61	    )
62	  }
63	
64	  if (!filtered.length) return NextResponse.json([])
65	
66	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(filtered.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
67	
68	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
69	    .from('farmers')
70	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method')
71	    .in('id', farmerIds)
72	    .eq('active', true)
73	
74	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
75	
76	  const result = filtered
77	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
78	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
79	
80	  return NextResponse.json(result)
81	}
82	
```

```json
  "old_string": "/* ─── Loading screen ────────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction LoadingScreen() {",
  "new_string": "/* ─── Farm images card ──────────────────────────────────────── */\ntype MediaPhoto = { id: string; url: string; caption?: string | null }\n\nfunction FarmImagesCard({ farmerId }: { farmerId: string }) {\n  const [photos, setPhotos] = useState<MediaPhoto[]>([])\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(null)\n  const [pendingFile, setPendingFile] = useState<File | null>(null)\n  const [caption, setCaption] = useState('')\n\n  const loadPhotos = useCallback(async () => {\n    setLoading(true)\n    const { data } = await supabase\n      .from('media')\n      .select('id, url, caption')\n      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)\n      .eq('type', 'photo')\n      .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })\n    setPhotos((data ?? []) as MediaPhoto[])\n    setLoading(false)\n  }, [farmerId])\n\n  useEffect(() => { loadPhotos() }, [loadPhotos])\n\n  const handleFile = (file: File | null) => {\n    setError('')\n    if (!file) return\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {\n      setError('Please pick an image file / చిత్రం ఫైల్ ఎంచుకోండి')\n      return\n    }\n    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) {\n      setError('Image is too large (max 8 MB) / చిత్రం 8 MB కంటే ఎక్కువ')\n      return\n    }\n    setPendingFile(file)\n    const reader = new FileReader()\n    reader.onload = (e) => setPreview(e.target?.result as string)\n    reader.readAsDataURL(file)\n  }\n\n  const handleUpload = async () => {\n    if (!pendingFile) return\n    setUploading(true)\n    setError('')\n    try {\n      const ext = pendingFile.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n      const path = `${farmerId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}.${ext}`\n      const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n        .from('farm-images')\n        .upload(path, pendingFile, {\n          cacheControl: '3600',\n          contentType: pendingFile.type,\n          upsert: false,\n        })\n      if (upErr) throw upErr\n\n      const { data: pub } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)\n      const publicUrl = pub.publicUrl\n\n      const { error: insErr } = await supabase.from('media').insert({\n        farmer_id: farmerId,\n        type: 'photo',\n        url: publicUrl,\n        caption: caption.trim() || null,\n        sort_order: photos.length,\n      })\n      if (insErr) throw insErr\n\n      setPreview(null)\n      setPendingFile(null)\n      setCaption('')\n      await loadPhotos()\n    } catch (e) {\n      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Upload failed'\n      setError(msg)\n    } finally {\n      setUploading(false)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const handleCancel = () => {\n    setPreview(null)\n    setPendingFile(null)\n    setCaption('')\n    setError('')\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3\">\n      <div>\n        <h2 className=\"font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight\">\n          Farm photos / పొలం ఫోటోలు\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n          Show buyers your farm / కొనుగోలుదారులకు మీ పొలం చూపించండి\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      {!preview && (\n        <label className=\"block w-full border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-5 px-4 text-center cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50\">\n          <div className=\"text-3xl mb-1\">📸</div>\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-bold text-green-800\">\n            Upload photo / ఫోటో అప్‌లోడ్\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5\">\n            Gallery or camera / గ్యాలరీ లేదా కెమెరా\n          </p>\n          <input\n            type=\"file\"\n            accept=\"image/*\"\n            capture=\"environment\"\n            className=\"hidden\"\n            onChange={(e) => handleFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}\n          />\n        </label>\n      )}\n\n      {preview && (\n        <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n          <div className=\"rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200\">\n            {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n            <img src={preview} alt=\"Preview\" className=\"w-full max-h-64 object-cover\" />\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"Caption (optional) / శీర్షిక\"\n            value={caption}\n            onChange={(e) => setCaption(e.target.value)}\n            className=\"w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none\"\n          />\n          {error && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2\">{error}</p>\n          )}\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n            <button\n              onClick={handleCancel}\n              disabled={uploading}\n              className=\"flex-1 border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm\"\n            >\n              Cancel / రద్దు\n            </button>\n            <button\n              onClick={handleUpload}\n              disabled={uploading}\n              className=\"flex-1 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-60\"\n            >\n              {uploading ? 'Uploading... / అప్‌లోడ్' : 'Save / సేవ్'}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {!preview && error && (\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2\">{error}</p>\n      )}\n\n      {loading ? (\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-3\">Loading photos...</p>\n      ) : photos.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-2\">\n          No photos yet / ఇంకా ఫోటోలు లేవు\n        </p>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5\">\n          {photos.map((p) => (\n            <div\n              key={p.id}\n              className=\"aspect-square rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 bg-gray-50\"\n            >\n              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n              <img\n                src={p.url}\n                alt={p.caption ?? ''}\n                className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n              />\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\n/* ─── Loading screen ────────────────────────────────────────── */\nfunction LoadingScreen() {"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/LanguageContext.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { createContext, useContext, ReactNode } from 'react'\nimport { t, bilingual, Language, TranslationKey } from './translations'\n\ntype LanguageContextType = {\n  lang: Language\n  setLang: (lang: Language) => void\n  tx: typeof t.en\n  bi: (key: TranslationKey) => string\n}\n\nconst biProxy = new Proxy({} as typeof t.en, {\n  get: (_, prop: string) => bilingual(prop as TranslationKey),\n}) as typeof t.en\n\nconst LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType>({\n  lang: 'en',\n  setLang: () => {},\n  tx: biProxy,\n  bi: (key) => bilingual(key),\n})\n\nexport function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <LanguageContext.Provider\n      value={{\n        lang: 'en',\n        setLang: () => {},\n        tx: biProxy,\n        bi: (key) => bilingual(key),\n      }}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </LanguageContext.Provider>\n  )\n}\n\nexport function useLang() {\n  return useContext(LanguageContext)\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/lib/translations.ts",
  "old_string": "export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof t.en",
  "new_string": "export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof t.en\n\nexport function bilingual(key: TranslationKey): string {\n  const en = (t.en as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''\n  const te = (t.te as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''\n  if (!te || en === te) return en\n  // Long / multi-line text (e.g. WhatsApp templates, descriptions) → stack with separator.\n  if (en.length > 60 || en.includes('\\n') || te.includes('\\n')) {\n    return `${en}\\n\\n---\\n\\n${te}`\n  }\n  return `${en} / ${te}`\n}"
}
```

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
5	import RegionMap from './RegionMap'
6	import FarmersTab from './tabs/FarmersTab'
7	import BrowseProduceTab from './tabs/BrowseProduceTab'
8	import AddFarmerTab from './tabs/AddFarmerTab'
9	
10	const CATEGORY_MAP: Record<string, string[]> = {
11	  Vegetables: ['Tomato', 'Brinjal', 'Drumstick', 'Bitter Gourd', 'Raw Banana'],
12	  Fruits: ['Mango', 'Banana', 'Papaya', 'Guava'],
13	  'Leafy greens': ['Palakura', 'Spinach', 'Methi', 'Coriander'],
14	}
15	
16	export default function RegionContent({
17	  farmers,
18	  produce,
19	}: {
20	  farmers: Record<string, unknown>[]
21	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
22	}) {
23	  const { tx } = useLang()
24	  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('All')
25	  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(0)
26	  const [highlightedFarmerId, setHighlightedFarmerId] = useState<string | null>(null)
27	
28	  const FILTERS = [tx.all, tx.vegetables, tx.fruits, tx.leafyGreens, tx.naturalOnly]
29	  const TABS = [tx.farmers, tx.browseProduce, tx.addFarmer]
30	
31	  // Map filter label back to English key for category lookup
32	  const filterKeyMap: Record<string, string> = {
33	    [tx.all]: 'All',
34	    [tx.vegetables]: 'Vegetables',
35	    [tx.fruits]: 'Fruits',
36	    [tx.leafyGreens]: 'Leafy greens',
37	    [tx.naturalOnly]: 'Natural only',
38	  }
39	
40	  const filterKey = filterKeyMap[activeFilter] ?? activeFilter
41	
42	  const filteredProduce = produce.filter((p) => {
43	    if (filterKey === 'All') return true
44	    if (filterKey === 'Natural only') return p.method === 'natural'
45	    const names = CATEGORY_MAP[filterKey] ?? []
46	    return names.some((n) => (p.name as string).includes(n))
47	  })
48	
49	  const filteredFarmerIds = new Set(filteredProduce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))
50	  const filteredFarmers = filterKey === 'All'
51	    ? farmers
52	    : farmers.filter((f) => filteredFarmerIds.has(f.id))
53	
54	  return (
55	    <div>
56	      {/* Filter chips */}
57	      <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">
58	        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
59	          {FILTERS.map((filter) => (
60	            <button
61	              key={filter}
62	              onClick={() => setActiveFilter(filter)}
63	              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
64	                activeFilter === filter
65	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
66	                  : 'bg-white text-gray-600 border-gray-200'
67	              }`}
68	            >
69	              {filter}
70	            </button>
71	          ))}
72	        </div>
73	      </div>
74	
75	      {/* SVG Map */}
76	      <RegionMap
77	        farmers={filteredFarmers}
78	        highlightedFarmerId={highlightedFarmerId}
79	        onPinClick={setHighlightedFarmerId}
80	      />
81	
82	      {/* Tabs */}
83	      <div className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white border-b border-gray-200">
84	        <div className="flex">
85	          {TABS.map((tab, i) => (
86	            <button
87	              key={tab}
88	              onClick={() => setActiveTab(i)}
89	              className={`flex-1 py-3 text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 transition-colors ${
90	                activeTab === i
91	                  ? 'border-green-700 text-green-700'
92	                  : 'border-transparent text-gray-500'
93	              }`}
94	            >
95	              {tab}
96	            </button>
97	          ))}
98	        </div>
99	      </div>
100	
101	      <div className="px-4 py-4">
102	        {activeTab === 0 && (
103	          <FarmersTab
104	            farmers={filteredFarmers}
105	            produce={produce}
106	            highlightedFarmerId={highlightedFarmerId}
107	          />
108	        )}
109	        {activeTab === 1 && <BrowseProduceTab produce={filteredProduce} farmers={farmers} />}
110	        {activeTab === 2 && <AddFarmerTab farmerCount={farmers.length} />}
111	      </div>
112	    </div>
113	  )
114	}
115
```

```
3	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'

5	type Review = {
6	  id: string
7	  reviewer_name: string
8	  reviewer_location?: string
9	  star_rating: number
10	  review_text?: string
11	  produce_ordered?: string
12	  created_at: string
13	}
14	
15	export default function ReviewsTab({ reviews }: { reviews: Record<string, unknown>[] }) {
16	  const { tx } = useLang()
17	  const list = reviews as Review[]
18	
19	  if (list.length === 0) {
20	    return <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noReviews}</div>
21	  }
22	
23	  const avg = list.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.star_rating, 0) / list.length
24	  const counts = [5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map((star) => ({
25	    star,
26	    count: list.filter((r) => r.star_rating === star).length,
27	  }))
28	
29	  return (
30	    <div className="space-y-5">
31	      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
32	        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
33	          <div className="text-center">
34	            <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-gray-900">{avg.toFixed(1)}</p>
35	            <p className="text-yellow-500 text-lg">{'★'.repeat(Math.round(avg))}</p>
36	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{list.length} {tx.reviews}</p>
37	          </div>
38	          <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
39	            {counts.map(({ star, count }) => (
40	              <div key={star} className="flex items-center gap-2">
41	                <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 w-3">{star}</span>
42	                <div className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-2">
43	                  <div className="bg-yellow-400 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ width: list.length ? `${(count / list.length) * 100}%` : '0%' }} />
44	                </div>
45	                <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 w-4">{count}</span>
46	              </div>
47	            ))}
48	          </div>
49	        </div>
50	      </div>
51	
52	      <div className="space-y-3">
53	        {list.map((review) => (
54	          <div key={review.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
55	            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
56	              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
57	                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-green-100 flex items-center justify-center text-green-800 font-bold text-sm flex-shrink-0">
58	                  {review.reviewer_name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
59	                </div>
60	                <div>
61	                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">{review.reviewer_name}</p>
62	                  {review.reviewer_location && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{review.reviewer_location}</p>}
63	                </div>
64	              </div>
65	              <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
66	                <p className="text-yellow-500 text-sm">{'★'.repeat(review.star_rating)}</p>
67	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
68	                  {new Date(review.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })}
69	                </p>
70	              </div>
71	            </div>
72	            {review.review_text && <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-3 leading-relaxed">{review.review_text}</p>}
73	            {review.produce_ordered && (
74	              <p className="text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold mt-2">{tx.bought} {review.produce_ordered}</p>
75	            )}
76	          </div>
77	        ))}
78	      </div>
79	    </div>
80	  )
81	}
82
```

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import Link from 'next/link'
4	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
5	
6	type Farmer = {
7	  id: string
8	  slug: string
9	  name: string
10	  village?: string
11	  district?: string
12	  method?: string
13	  rating_avg?: number
14	  farming_since_year?: number
15	}
16	
17	type Produce = {
18	  farmer_id: string
19	  name: string
20	  emoji?: string
21	}
22	
23	export default function FarmersTab({
24	  farmers,
25	  produce,
26	  highlightedFarmerId,
27	}: {
28	  farmers: Record<string, unknown>[]
29	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
30	  highlightedFarmerId: string | null
31	}) {
32	  const { tx } = useLang()
33	  const list = farmers as Farmer[]
34	  const produceList = produce as Produce[]
35	
36	  if (list.length === 0) {
37	    return <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noFarmersFound}</div>
38	  }
39	
40	  return (
41	    <div className="space-y-3">
42	      {list.map((farmer, index) => {
43	        const farmerProduce = produceList.filter((p) => p.farmer_id === farmer.id)
44	        const isHighlighted = highlightedFarmerId === farmer.id
45	        const isCofounder = index === 0
46	        const initials = farmer.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()
47	        const yearsfarming = farmer.farming_since_year
48	          ? new Date().getFullYear() - farmer.farming_since_year
49	          : null
50	
51	        return (
52	          <div key={farmer.id}
53	            className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-4 transition-all ${
54	              isHighlighted ? 'border-green-500 shadow-md' : 'border-gray-100'
55	            } ${isCofounder ? 'border-l-4 border-l-green-600' : ''}`}
56	          >
57	            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
58	              <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-green-700 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold flex-shrink-0">
59	                {initials}
60	              </div>
61	              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
62	                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
63	                  <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm">{farmer.name}</h3>
64	                  {isCofounder && (
65	                    <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
66	                      {tx.cofounder}
67	                    </span>
68	                  )}
69	                  {farmer.rating_avg === 0 && (
70	                    <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
71	                      {tx.newFarmer}
72	                    </span>
73	                  )}
74	                </div>
75	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
76	                  {farmer.village ?? farmer.district}
77	                  {yearsfarming ? ` · ${yearsfarming} ${tx.yrsFarming}` : ''}
78	                </p>
79	
80	                {farmerProduce.length > 0 && (
81	                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-2">
82	                    {farmerProduce.slice(0, 4).map((p) => (
83	                      <span key={p.name} className="bg-green-50 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
84	                        {p.emoji ?? '🌿'} {p.name}
85	                      </span>
86	                    ))}
87	                    {farmerProduce.length > 4 && (
88	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">+{farmerProduce.length - 4} more</span>
89	                    )}
90	                  </div>
91	                )}
92	
93	                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
94	                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
95	                    {farmer.method === 'natural' && (
96	                      <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
97	                        {tx.naturalFarming}
98	                      </span>
99	                    )}
100	                    {farmer.rating_avg ? (
101	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">⭐ {farmer.rating_avg}</span>
102	                    ) : null}
103	                  </div>
104	                  <Link href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}`}
105	                    className="bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">
106	                    {tx.viewShop}
107	                  </Link>
108	                </div>
109	              </div>
110	            </div>
111	          </div>
112	        )
113	      })}
114	
115	      <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-center">
116	        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">{tx.knowAFarmer}</p>
117	        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{tx.helpThemReach}</p>
118	        <button
119	          onClick={() => {
120	            const tab = document.querySelectorAll('button[class*="border-b-2"]')
121	            if (tab[2]) (tab[2] as HTMLButtonElement).click()
122	          }}
123	          className="mt-3 bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-600 text-sm font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg"
124	        >
125	          {tx.addFarmerCta}
126	        </button>
127	      </div>
128	    </div>
129	  )
130	}
131
```

## Code changes

- **Rename**: `neighbourhood` → `local` in `consumer/page.tsx` + `translations.ts`.
- **Hero**: wrapped in `max-w-3xl mx-auto`, same for search card, chips, lists, demand banner — no more desktop stretch.
- **Pickup locations**:
  - Farmer dashboard profile modal has an add/remove chip editor.
  - Farmer profile produce tab + cart sheet show a dropdown; selection is injected into the WhatsApp pre-fill.
  - API routes now return `pickup_locations` with the farmer object; cart stores it per-item.
- **Description column**: already wired on the publish form; now rendered on each `ProduceCard` in the farmer profile.
- **Image upload** (dashboard → "Farm photos / పొలం ఫోటోలు" card):
  - `<input accept="image/*" capture="environment">` — gallery or rear camera.
  - Preview before upload, optional caption, upload to `farm-images` bucket, insert into `media` table, gallery refreshes.
- **Bilingual**: `useLang().tx` is now a Proxy that returns `"English / Telugu"` for every key (multi-line templates use `\n\n---\n\n`). All existing `tx.X` sites became bilingual for free. Manually bilingualized inline literal strings across consumer page, farmer dashboard, login, profile components. `LanguageToggle` is now a no-op.

`npm run build` passes clean.
